Hi. Um, well, I came in because... people have been noticing a change in how I move lately. It's kind of two extremes.

I just can't get it out of my head that it's doing exactly that.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the belief about the ticketing software tracking you is true, even when others tell you it's not possible?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I do. I mean, people have told me it doesn't work like that, but... I just can't shake it. Like I said, even if I'm having a decent week and feeling okay, I still get completely convinced that the old ticketing software is tracking me across all those job sites and telling people I'm unreliable. Like it's following me through different job sites online and flagging my profile as unreliable to other employers. I just can't seem to shake the thought that it's doing that.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe the ticketing software is actually capable of tracking you in the way you've described?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I do believe it. I know it sounds a little out there, but even when I'm having a completely fine week, I just... I get so convinced that it's happening. Like the software from my old customer service job is actually tracking me across different job sites and flagging me as an unreliable worker to everyone else. I just can't get it out of my head that it's doing exactly that.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the belief about the ticketing software tracking you is true, even when others tell you it's not possible?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I do. I mean, people have told me it doesn't work like that, but... I just can't shake it. Like I said, even if I'm having a decent week and feeling okay, I still get completely convinced that the old ticketing software is tracking me across all those job sites and telling people I'm unreliable. It just feels too real to ignore, no matter what anyone else says.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the ticketing software is tracking you specifically, or is it also tracking other people?\n\nPatient: I think it's just me. I mean, I haven't really thought about it tracking anyone else. It's just... like I've been saying, even when I'm having a totally normal, okay week, I just get this overwhelming feeling that it's focused on me. That the software from that old job is specifically following my activity on those job boards and making sure people think I'm unreliable. I don't know why it would care about anyone else.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices or seen things that other people cannot?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I have. Um... sometimes I hear the beep from my old work headset. Or I'll hear a voice that sounds like a supervisor saying my name, or telling me I'm being too slow. It happens when my laptop is completely closed and there is no phone ringing anywhere around me, but I still hear it.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the ticketing software tracking you is part of a larger plan or scheme, or is it just focused on you being unreliable?\n\nPatient: I don't really know about a larger plan. I just know it's focused on me. Like I mentioned before, even if I'm having a pretty good week and my mood is fine, I still get completely convinced that this old ticketing software is tracking me through all these different job sites. It just feels like it's out there, flagging me as an unreliable worker to anyone who might hire me. I don't know why, but that's what's happening.\n\nDoctor: Do you think there are other things going on, besides the ticketing software, that are all connected and part of why it's tracking you?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't really noticed anything else like that. It's really just the software thing. I haven't seen any other connections or anything else weird going on. Just what I've told you about the job boards.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the ticketing software tracking you fits into a larger story or explanation that makes sense to you?\n\nPatient: Um, no, not really. There isn't some big story behind it that I can think of. I just know it's happening. I don't really have a good explanation for why it's set up that way... it just is.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the ticketing software tracking you is part of a system or network that is working together to achieve something?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think it's part of some big network or system working together. It's really just the software itself. I haven't seen any evidence of a whole group of things coordinating against me or anything like that. Just the software flagging me on the job sites.\n\nDoctor: Would you say that the idea of the ticketing software tracking you is something that could happen in real life?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I mean... it is happening to me, so it definitely happens in real life. I'm not really sure about all the technical details of exactly how they do it, but companies track data and use software all the time. So yeah, it's definitely something that's possible.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the ticketing software tracking you is all part of a single, larger idea that makes sense to you?\n\nPatient: Not really, no. I think it's kind of like what I was saying a minute ago... there isn't some big master plan or overarching idea behind it. It's really just the software itself doing it. I don't see any grand design to it, it's just what's happening to me at work.\n\nDoctor: Do you find it difficult to express your thoughts in a logical and coherent manner when talking about the ticketing software tracking you?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I feel like I'm explaining it to you okay right now. I don't get all jumbled up or confused when I talk about it, if that's what you mean. It's just a thing that's happening at my job, so I just... explain what I see going on. It's pretty straightforward to me.\n\nDoctor: Do you often find yourself losing your train of thought when talking about things other than the ticketing software?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. I feel like I can hold a normal conversation about whatever just fine.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
